---
// 物品注册表
export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen'
  | 'obsidianSkin' | 'gills' | 'manaRegen' | 'magicPower' | 'featherfall' | 'waterWalking' | 'archery' | 'nightOwl';
// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;

export interface ItemDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  maxStack: number;
  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
  placeStyle?: number;     // 放置的样式索引(vanilla style 帧 X 偏移 = style*fw*18)
  magic?: {               // 魔法武器:消耗 mana 发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    manaCost: number;
    projSpeed: number;
    proj?: 'spark' | 'bolt'; // 投射物外观
  };
  ranged?: {              // 远程武器:消耗 ammo 弹药发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    knockback: number;
    ammo: string;         // 弹药 item key(箭)
    projSpeed: number;    // 投射物速度 px/tick
    /** 原版 item id（PickAmmo/ItemCheck_Shoot 数值以 vanilla-itemcombat.json 为准） */
    vid?: number;
  };
  /** 原版 AmmoID（40=箭；弹药物品自身标识，PickAmmo 匹配弓 useAmmo） */
  ammoId?: number;
  /** 原版 item id（战斗数值数据源） */
  vid?: number;
  wall?: string;          // 放置的 wall key
  /** 原版 createWall（Item.cs SetDefaults）：放置的背景墙 vanilla id。
   *  vanilla-wallitems.json 全量 124 项（tools/extract-wallitems.mjs 提取） */
  wallId?: number;
  tool?: {
    type: ToolType;
    power?: number;       // 工具力（镐/斧/锤）
    damage?: number;      // 武器伤害
    speed?: number;       // 挥砍动画 tick（≈原版 useAnimation）
    /** 原版 useTime（Item.cs SetDefaults）：挖掘每击冷却——原版挖掘节奏的真基准，
     *  铜 15-23 / 铁 13-20 / 银 11-19 / 金 17-23（applyItemTime 冷却=useTime） */
    useTime?: number;
    knockback?: number;
    reach?: number;       // 攻击/作用半径（px）
  };
  /** 副工具力（Hamaxe 系双工具：主 tool.type 外的另一力，挖掘入口按主类型判定） */
  axePower?: number;
  heal?: number;          // 食用/饮用回复
  potion?: {
    buff: PotionBuff;
    duration: number;     // 秒
    isHealType: boolean;  // 受"耐药性"封锁
  };
  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果
  armor?: { slot: ArmorSlot; defense: number };   // 盔甲（可穿装备/时装格，仅装备格计防御）
  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）
  wireTool?: {            // 电路工具（原版 Player.cs:30289-30444 ItemCheck_UseWiringTools）
    place?: number;       // 单击放置的导线位掩码(TOOL_RED/BLUE/GREEN/YELLOW/ACTUATOR)
    cutter?: boolean;     // 剪线钳:按优先级移除一件(致动器>黄>绿>蓝>红)
    rod?: boolean;        // 致动魔杖:手动翻转致动状态
    grand?: boolean;      // 宏伟蓝图:拖拽批量(R 键切模式)
  };
  /** 油漆 id（Item.paint，Item.cs:118；SetDefaults 13374-13535/21168-21185 赋值）。
   *  1073-1099 → 1-27、1966/1967/1968 → 28/29/30（PaintID.cs）。0/缺省=非油漆。
   *  Item 上无 paintWall 字段——刷墙/刷块由工具决定（Player.cs:41236/:41254） */
  paint?: number;
  /** 油漆工具族（Player.cs PlaceThing_Paintbrush/Roller/Scrapper :41254/:41236/:41152）：
   *  brush=1071/1543 刷方块、roller=1072/1544 刷墙、scraper=1100/1545 清除 */
  paintTool?: 'brush' | 'roller' | 'scraper';
  desc?: string;
}

export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
const byKey: Record<string, number> = {};
export const ITEM_BY_KEY: Record<string, number> = byKey;

// damage/knockBack/useTime/useAnimation/shoot/useAmmo/shootSpeed/ammo/consumable/noMelee/autoReuse/mana
import combatJson from './vanilla-itemcombat.json';
import { projectileData, AI_ANCHORED, AI_BOOMERANG, AI_FLAIL, AI_GLOWSTICK, AI_GRENADE, AI_SPEAR, AI_SHORTSWORD, AI_THROWN, AI_YOYO } from './vanillaProjectiles';

export interface ItemCombat {
  damage?: number;
  crit?: number;           // Item.crit（武器自带暴击，GetWeaponCrit P:25230-25232 消费）
  knockBack?: number;
  useTime?: number;
  useAnimation?: number;
  shoot?: number;
  useAmmo?: number;
  shootSpeed?: number;
  ammo?: number;
  consumable?: boolean;
  noMelee?: boolean;
  autoReuse?: boolean;
  mana?: number;
  melee?: boolean;
  ranged?: boolean;
  magic?: boolean;
  summon?: boolean;
  /** Item.cs useTurn=true（1141 件，工具族；武器 false）——itemAnimation 窗内移动改向豁免（:19546） */
  useTurn?: boolean;
}

const RAW = combatJson as unknown as Record<string, ItemCombat>;

// ---- 鞭系（DefaultToWhip 提取，Item.cs SetDefaults switch 共 18 把）----
import whipsJson from './vanilla-whips.json';
const WHIPS = whipsJson as unknown as Record<string, { proj: number; dmg: number; kb: number; speed: number; anim: number }>;

// ---- 提取缺口补丁（tools/extract-itemcombat.mjs 只解析 case 内字面赋值）----
// DefaultToSpear/DefaultToStaff/DefaultToMagicWeapon/DefaultToRangedWeapon 的形参
// 赋值（Item.cs:47630/:48045/:48057）漏提：4347/4348/4703/5629 仅剩 damage/knockBack、
// 4788-4790（剑矛三件 DefaultToSpear+SetWeaponValues）与 4060-4062 整条缺失。
// 下表按源码 case 逐件 1:1（:35651/:35672/:37119/:38629/:39341-39353/:44212）：
//   DefaultToSpear → useStyle5 / useTime=useAnimation=animationTime / shootSpeed=push / noMelee+melee
//   DefaultToStaff → magic / autoReuse / mana=manaPerShot / UseSound43
//   DefaultToMagicWeapon → magic / noMelee / useTime=useAnimation=singleShotTime
//   DefaultToRangedWeapon → noMelee / ranged / useAmmo=ammoID（5629 随后 ranged=false 已在表内）
const EXTRACT_PATCH: Record<string, Partial<ItemCombat>> = {
  '4060': { shoot: 728, useAmmo: 75, useTime: 18, useAnimation: 18, shootSpeed: 20, damage: 60, knockBack: 5, noMelee: true, ranged: true, autoReuse: true },
  '4061': { shoot: 730, useTime: 28, useAnimation: 28, shootSpeed: 3.5, damage: 14, knockBack: 6, noMelee: true, melee: true },
  '4062': { shoot: 731, useTime: 17, useAnimation: 17, shootSpeed: 8, mana: 6, damage: 24, knockBack: 3, noMelee: true, magic: true, autoReuse: true },
  '4347': { shoot: 876, useTime: 36, useAnimation: 36, shootSpeed: 15, noMelee: true, magic: true },
  '4348': { shoot: 876, useTime: 36, useAnimation: 36, shootSpeed: 15, noMelee: true, magic: true },
  '4703': { shoot: 14, useAmmo: 97, useTime: 55, useAnimation: 55, shootSpeed: 7, noMelee: true, ranged: true },
  '4788': { shoot: 877, useTime: 24, useAnimation: 24, shootSpeed: 3.5, damage: 60, knockBack: 12, noMelee: true, melee: true },
  '4789': { shoot: 878, useTime: 24, useAnimation: 24, shootSpeed: 3.5, damage: 130, knockBack: 14, noMelee: true, melee: true },
  '4790': { shoot: 879, useTime: 24, useAnimation: 24, shootSpeed: 3.5, damage: 90, knockBack: 13, noMelee: true, melee: true },
  '5629': { shoot: 1088, useAmmo: 27, useTime: 20, useAnimation: 20, shootSpeed: 10, noMelee: true, autoReuse: true },
  // ---- 七金属短剑 SetDefaults1(6) 模板漏提(Item.cs:1377-1392;2026-08-13
  //   "铜剑变投掷"根因:shootSpeed 缺→默认 8→位移 120px 像丢剑;autoReuse 应 false;
  //   noMelee 应 true。铁短剑 item 6 自带全量(不走模板)不受影响) ----
  '3483': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3489': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3495': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3501': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3507': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3513': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
  '3519': { shootSpeed: 2.1, noMelee: true, autoReuse: false },
};

const TABLE: Record<string, ItemCombat> = { ...RAW };
for (const k of Object.keys(EXTRACT_PATCH)) {
  TABLE[k] = { ...(RAW[k] ?? {}), ...EXTRACT_PATCH[k] } as ItemCombat;
}

/** 原版 item id → 战斗数据（无条目返回 null） */
export function itemCombat(vanillaId: number): ItemCombat | null {
  return TABLE[String(vanillaId)] ?? null;
}

/** 弓类 AmmoID.Arrow = 40（AmmoID.cs:116） */
export const AMMO_ARROW = 40;
/** AmmoID 常量（AmmoID.cs:114-146）：子弹/凝胶/钱币/坠落之星/沙/飞镖/火箭/溶液/信号弹 */
export const AMMO_GEL = 23;
export const AMMO_COIN = 71;
export const AMMO_FALLEN_STAR = 75;
export const AMMO_BULLET = 97;
export const AMMO_SAND = 169;
export const AMMO_DART = 283;
export const AMMO_ROCKET = 771;
export const AMMO_SOLUTION = 780;
export const AMMO_FLARE = 931;

// ================= vi_* 武器语义解析（1456 数据驱动） =================

/** vi_<id>_<slug> key → 原版 item id（vi_ 批次未显式存 vid 时从 key 反解） */
export function viIdFromKey(key: string): number {
  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

export type CombatWeapon =
  | { kind: 'melee'; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; useAnimation: number; autoReuse: boolean; shoot?: number; shootSpeed?: number }
  | { kind: 'summon'; shoot: number; sentry: boolean; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; shootSpeed: number }
  | { kind: 'whip'; shoot: number; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; shootSpeed: number; itemId: number }
  | {
      kind: 'boomerang' | 'spear' | 'yoyo' | 'flail' | 'grenade' | 'glowstick' | 'magic' | 'shot' | 'summon';
      shoot: number;
      damage: number;
      knockback: number;
      useTime: number;
      shootSpeed: number;
      mana?: number;
      sentry?: boolean;
    };

  /** 溢出缓冲长度（Game 的生成期轮上限计算用，原版 LiquidBuffer.numLiquidBuffer） */

  private idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.world.store.w + x; }

  private blocksLiquid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.world.store;

  private solidTileFull(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return true;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
    return this.solidNP[st.type[i]] === 1 && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
  }

  // ================= AddWater（Liquid.cs:835-872） =================

  addWater(x: number, y: number) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (x >= st.w - 5 || y >= st.h - 5 || x < 5 || y < 5) return;
    const i = this.idx(x, y);
    if (this.checking[i] || st.liquid[i] === 0) return;
    const t = st.type[i];
    if (t !== 0 && st.flags[i]) {
      if (this.solidNP[t] === 1) return;
    }
    if (this.numLiquid >= this.curMaxLiquid - 1) {
      // 原版走 LiquidBuffer 缓冲、清扫轮回灌（Liquid.cs:840-843），超缓冲上限才丢
      if (this.bufTail - this.bufHead < LiquidSim.BUFFER_CAP) {
        if (this.bufTail === LiquidSim.BUFFER_CAP) {
          // ★到顶且 head>0:前移压缩(原版 Array.shift 后 push 可重新装满;曾漏此步
          // → 越界写静默丢条目,与原版容量语义不等价,A/B 哈希报警)

Now I have the exact current state. Let me write the new implementation.

Key design:
- Keep the doc comment but expand it for the full family.
- Restructure useBucket into the vanilla two-branch shape (scoop / pour), covering all 12 items.

Important details to preserve from vanilla:

**Entry gates (:45706, :45715)**:
- inBounds (repo)
- reach: IsInTileInteractionRange(Simple, tileBoost) → inTileRange(tx, ty, TILE_BOOST) — I'll add per-family tileBoost: 4820/4872/5302/5303/5364/3031/3032 = +2, 5304 = +3, others 0 (Item.cs:28052/:39452/:39648/:42042/:42054/:42534 tileBoost+=2; 5304 :42062 tileBoost+=3).
- ItemTimeIsZero + controlUseItem → handled at the dispatch line (add `inp.mouseDown && this.player.useTime === 0`).

**Scoop branch entry (:45719)** with repo encoding (WATER=1, LAVA=2, HONEY=3, SHIMMER=4):
```
(205 && t !== SHIMMER) || (3032 && (t===WATER || t===SHIMMER)) || (4872 && t===LAVA) || (5303 && t===HONEY) || 5304
```

**Scoop gate (:45733)**: `liquid <= 0 || (sum3x3 <= 100 && !sponge)` → return.

**Swap (:45738-45759)** for 205 only: honey→1128, lava→207, shimmer→return, else→206. Sponges: no swap.

**Scoop exec (:45760-45809)**:
- sound 19, ApplyItemTime → useTime = itemCombat(vid)?.useTime ?? 10
- num3 = liquid[i]; setLiquid(tx,ty,0,0)
- if num3 >= 255 return
- drain 3×3 (excl center) same-type neighbors up to 255 total

**Pour branch (:45811-45888)**:
- gate: liquid >= 200 || (nactive && solid && !solidTop && type!=546) → return
- 207|4820 → LAVA: liquid==0 || t===LAVA → pour; consume unless 4820
- 206|3031 → WATER: liquid==0 || t===WATER → pour; consume unless 3031
- 1128|5302 → HONEY: liquid==0 || t===HONEY → pour; consume unless 5302
- 5364 → SHIMMER: liquid==0 || t===SHIMMER → pour; never consume

Note: the pour sub-branch condition `liquid == 0 || type == X` is what vanilla checks AFTER the ≥200/solid gate. Equivalent repo form: `st.liquid[i] === 0 || tHere === lt`. Note: when liquid[i]>0 but <200 and same type → pour (tops up to 255). Yes vanilla does that too.

Wait, one subtlety: vanilla pour gate at :45813 checks `liquid >= 200` FIRST, then each sub-branch requires `liquid == 0 || liquidType == X`. So a tile with liquid 100 of DIFFERENT type → sub-branch condition false → nothing happens (no return, just no pour). Same effect as repo's early return.

Another subtlety: for the solid check, vanilla uses `nactive() && tileSolid[type] && !tileSolidTop[type] && type != 546`. The repo: `st.flags[i] && td && td.solid && !td.platform && td.vanilla?.sheet !== 546`. Keep the repo approximation.

Now the "微光桶另有转化交互" question: does 5364 have any special shimmer interaction? Looking at :45877-45888 — it just pours shimmer 255, ApplyItemTime, no consumption. No special interaction in ItemCheck_UseBuckets. What about shimmer conversion when poured on the player/NPC? That's handled by the LiquidSim's ShimmerCheck (repo already has shimmer-transformation from the shimmer port memory). The pour itself is simple. So no deviation needed. 

Also should I check PutItemInInventoryFromItemUsage — vanilla stack-- then puts the new item. The repo swap replaces the slot with stack 1. Existing convention, keep.

One more thing: vanilla `ApplyItemTime` for autoReuse items — the itemAnimation is 12/15/8. The repo only tracks useTime. Consistent with the rest of the repo (e.g. thrown uses useTime). Fine.

Now, about `this.input.rightDown = false;` — I'm dropping it since the dispatch now requires mouseDown (left click). Actually hold on. Let me think again about whether adding the mouseDown gate could regress something. The bucket branch previously ran unconditionally every frame. Any test or probe that calls updateUse with a bucket held and expects pouring without mouseDown would break.   g.input.mouseY = (ty * 16 + 8 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cam.viewH / 2;
  const hp0 = p.hp;
  g.input.mouseDown = true;
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
  g.input.mouseDown = false;
  return { near: Math.abs(p.cx - (tx * 16 + 8)) < 40, hpDrop: hp0 - p.hp };
});

Now I'll replace the whole useBucket (9539-9623) with the new implementation. Let me write it.

```ts
  /** 桶/海绵族 12 件（Player.ItemCheck_UseBuckets，Player.cs:45704-45890 1:1）：
   *  族表（:45706）：205 空桶 / 206 水 / 207 岩浆 / 1128 蜂蜜 / 3031 无底水桶 /
   *    3032 超吸海绵 / 4820 无底岩浆桶 / 4872 岩浆海绵 / 5302 无底蜂蜜桶 /
   *    5303 蜂蜜海绵 / 5304 终极海绵 / 5364 无底微光桶
   *  舀取支入口（:45719）：205 且非微光 / 3032 水|微光 / 4872 岩浆 / 5303 蜂蜜 /
   *    5304 任意液体；合计门（:45733）海绵族豁免 ≤100；仅 205 换桶（:45738 消耗块
   *    显式排除 3032/4872/5303/5304——海绵族无饱和变体、不消耗）
   *  倾倒支（:45811-45888）：≥200 或 active 实心非 solidTop（546 例外）拒绝（:45813）；
   *    空格或同类才倒（:45819/:45839/:45859/:45877）；无底族不换桶；
   *    5364 微光桶即"倒微光 255 不消耗"，无额外转化交互（转化走液体侧 ShimmerCheck） */
  private useBucket(bucketVid: number, tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const st = this.world.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return;
    // 射程门（:45706 IsInTileInteractionRange Simple 档 + tileBoost）：无底桶/海绵族
    // tileBoost+=2（Item.cs:28052/:39452/:39648/:42042/:42053/:42534）、5304 +=3（:42062）
    const tb = bucketVid === 5304 ? 3
      : (bucketVid === 3031 || bucketVid === 3032 || bucketVid === 4820
        || bucketVid === 4872 || bucketVid === 5302 || bucketVid === 5303 || bucketVid === 5364) ? 2 : 0;
    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty, tb)) return;
    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
    // 原版 liquidType()==0 ≡ 水；本仓 +1 编码下 0 视同水
    const tHere = st.liquidType[i] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER;
    // 3×3 同类合计（:45723-45731）
    let sum3x3 = 0;
    for (let ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {
      for (let oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(tx + ox, ty + oy)) continue;
        const j = st.idx(tx + ox, ty + oy);
        if ((st.liquidType[j] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) === tHere) sum3x3 += st.liquid[j];
      }
    }
    // 倾倒实心门（:45813 nactive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && type!=546 的仓库近似）
    const td = st.type[i] !== 0 ? TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]] : undefined;
    const solidBlocked = !!st.flags[i] && !!td && !!td.solid && !td.platform
      && td.vanilla?.sheet !== 546;
    const op = Game.bucketUse(bucketVid, st.liquid[i], tHere, sum3x3, solidBlocked);
    if (op.kind === 'none') return;
    if (op.kind === 'pour') {
      st.setLiquid(tx, ty, 255, op.liquidType!);           // :45823/:45843/:45863/:45881
    } else {
      Game.bucketScoopDrain(st, tx, ty, tHere);            // :45762-45809
    }
    this.sfx.play('splash', 1, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8); // SoundID 19（:45760/:45821/:45841/:45861/:45879）
    if (op.swapTo !== undefined) {
      // 原版 sItem.stack-- + PutItemInInventoryFromItemUsage：手中原格替换（不跳格）
      this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = { id: ITEM_BY_KEY[VI(op.swapTo)], stack: 1 };
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
    }
    this.player.useTime = op.useTime;                      // ApplyItemTime（:45761/:45830 族）
  }

  /** 桶/海绵族决策纯函数（Player.cs:45719-45888）——输入目标格状态,输出动作。
   *  liquidType 用仓库 +1 编码（1 水/2 岩浆/3 蜂蜜/4 微光,0≡水）。
   *  kind: none=门拦下 / pour=倾倒 255 / scoop=舀取(含四邻回吸) */
  static bucketUse(vid: number, liquid: number, liquidType: number, sum3x3: number,
    solidBlocked: boolean): { kind: 'none' | 'pour' | 'scoop'; liquidType?: number; swapTo?: number; useTime: number } {
    const t = liquidType || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER;
    const isSponge = vid === 3032 || vid === 4872 || vid === 5303 || vid === 5304;
    // ---- 舀取支入口（:45719）----
    const scoop = (vid === 205 && t !== LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER)
      || (vid === 3032 && (t === LIQUID_TYPE.WATER || t === LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER))
      || (vid === 4872 && t === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA)
      || (vid === 5303 && t === LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY)
      || vid === 5304;                                      // 终极海绵任意液体
    if (scoop) {
      // 门（:45733）：无液体拒绝;合计 ≤100 且非海绵拒绝（海绵可吸浅洼）
      if (liquid <= 0 || (sum3x3 <= 100 && !isSponge)) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };
      // 换桶链（:45738-45759）：仅 205——蜜→1128/岩浆→207/水→206（微光已被入口拦）
      return {
        kind: 'scoop',
        swapTo: vid === 205 ? (t === LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY ? 1128 : t === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA ? 207 : 206) : undefined,
        useTime: itemCombat(vid)?.useTime ?? 10,            // ApplyItemTime（:45761）
      };
    }
    // ---- 倾倒支（:45811-45888）----
    if (liquid >= 200 || solidBlocked) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };  // :45813
    const pourType: Record<number, number> = {
      207: LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA, 4820: LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA,       // :45817-45835
      206: LIQUID_TYPE.WATER, 3031: LIQUID_TYPE.WATER,     // :45837-45855
      1128: LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY, 5302: LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY,    // :45857-45875
      5364: LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER,                           // :45877-45888
    };
    const lt = pourType[vid];
    if (lt === undefined) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 }; // 205 在微光上/海绵族不进此支
    if (liquid > 0 && t !== lt) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };  // 空格或同类才倒
    // 满桶族回空桶（:45827/:45847/:45867）;无底族 3031/4820/5302/5364 不消耗
    const bottomless = vid === 3031 || vid === 4820 || vid === 5302 || vid === 5364;
    return {
      kind: 'pour',
      liquidType: lt,
      swapTo: bottomless ? undefined : 205,
      useTime: itemCombat(vid)?.useTime ?? 10,              // ApplyItemTime（:45830/:45850/:45870/:45883）
    };
  }

  /** 舀取执行（:45762-45809）：清目标格;不足 255 时从 3×3 同类邻居回吸补足,
   *  余液留在地里（海绵同走此路——原版海绵与空桶共用同一舀取体,吸到 255 为止）。
   *  返回舀走总量。 */
  static bucketScoopDrain(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, tHere: number): number {
    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
    let got = st.liquid[i];
    st.setLiquid(tx, ty, 0, 0);
    if (got >= 255) return got;                             // :45775
    for (let ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {
      for (let oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
        if ((ox === 0 && oy === 0) || !st.inBounds(tx + ox, ty + oy)) continue;
        const j = st.idx(tx + ox, ty + oy);
        if (st.liquid[j] <= 0 || (st.liquidType[j] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) !== tHere) continue;
        const take = Math.min(st.liquid[j], 255 - got);
        if (take <= 0) continue;                            // 已吸满 255,余格不动
        got += take;
        st.setLiquid(tx + ox, ty + oy, st.liquid[j] - take, tHere);
      }
    }
    return got;
  }
```

Wait — one bug: in vanilla :45791, when taking 0 (num4 = 255 - num3 = 0), it still writes `liquidType(liquidType)` — a no-op. My `take <= 0 → continue` skips the setLiquid entirely — same net effect. Good.

Another check on vanilla drain: `num4 = Main.tile[k,l].liquid; if (num4 + num3 > 255) num4 = 255 - num3;` — so take = min(neighbor, 255-got). Same.

Now — one subtle thing about the vanilla scoop branch for the empty bucket 205: `sItem.stack--` then PutItemInInventoryFromItemUsage. If the bucket had stack >1... buckets maxStack is 9999 in 1.4.4+? Hmm — VANILLA_MAX_STACK has no entry for 205-207/1128, so 9999. Whatever — the repo swap replaces with stack 1, consistent with existing behavior. Not my concern.

Now double-check the `useTime` fallback: `?? 10`. itemCombat has entries for all 12 ids (verified). Fallback 10 = regular bucket useTime. Fine.

Hmm — one more consideration on `sum3x3` for the pour path: computed but unused in pour. Vanilla only computes it in the scoop branch. Computing always is harmless (9 array reads).

Wait, actually there IS one behavioral subtlety: vanilla's scoop branch computes `num` (target type) and `num2` (sum) BEFORE the gate — the sum only counts neighbors whose type equals the TARGET's type. Mine: same (tHere captured first). ✓.

Another subtlety in the pour branch I should double check — vanilla 207 pour condition: `liquid == 0 || liquidType() == 1` (lava). Repo: `liquid > 0 && t !== LAVA → none`. So pouring lava onto a tile with liquid 100 water → none. ✓ (Repo's old code had the same gate.)

And pouring WATER (206) onto a tile with lava 100: vanilla → `liquidType()==1 != 0` → no pour. ✓ Same.

Now — what about the old repo 3031 behavior of pouring onto any liquid <200? Fixed now (must be empty or water). This matches vanilla :45839.

Edge case: 5364 on a tile with liquid 255 shimmer → scoop? No: 5364 is not in the scoop entry (only 205/3032/4872/5303/5304). So pour path → liquid >= 200 → none. ✓ Vanilla: 5364 not in the :45719 condition → else branch → :45813 liquid>=200 → return. ✓

Edge: 4820 (bottomless lava) on water 100: scoop entry? No. Pour: liquid < 200, not solid; lt = LAVA; liquid > 0 && t(water) !== LAVA → none. ✓ vanilla :45819 `liquidType()==1` false → nothing. ✓

Edge: 5304 (ultra sponge) on lava: scoop entry true; gate: liquid>0 → ok (sponge bypasses sum); swapTo undefined; drain. ✓ vanilla: `sItem.type == 5304` → enters, no swap, scoops. ✓

Now the doc comment header for useBucket mentions old line refs; I'm replacing.

One more: `this.input.rightDown = false` removed — with the new dispatch gate requiring mouseDown, rightDown is irrelevant. Note: mouseDown is NOT cleared, so holding pours repeatedly every useTime ticks = vanilla autoReuse.

TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]] : undefined;\n      if (st.flags[i] && td && td.solid && !td.platform && td.vanilla?.sheet !== 546) return;\n      st.setLiquid(tx, ty, 255, lt);\n      this.sfx.play('splash', 1, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8); // SoundID 19（:45816/45833）\n      swap(205);\n    }\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** 桶/海绵族 12 件（Player.ItemCheck_UseBuckets，Player.cs:45704-45890 1:1）：\n   *  族表（:45706）：205 空桶 / 206 水 / 207 岩浆 / 1128 蜂蜜 / 3031 无底水桶 /\n   *    3032 超吸海绵 / 4820 无底岩浆桶 / 4872 岩浆海绵 / 5302 无底蜂蜜桶 /\n   *    5303 蜂蜜海绵 / 5304 终极海绵 / 5364 无底微光桶\n   *  舀取支入口（:45719）：205 且非微光 / 3032 水|微光 / 4872 岩浆 / 5303 蜂蜜 /\n   *    5304 任意液体；合计门（:45733）海绵族豁免；仅 205 换桶（:45738 消耗块\n   *    显式排除 3032/4872/5303/5304——海绵族不消耗、无饱和变体）\n   *  倾倒支（:45811-45888）：≥200 或 active 实心非 solidTop（546 例外）拒绝（:45813）；\n   *    空格或同类才倒（:45819/:45839/:45859/:45877）；无底族不换桶；\n   *    5364 = 倒微光 255 不消耗,无额外交互（实体转化走液体侧 ShimmerCheck） */\n  private useBucket(bucketVid: number, tx: number, ty: number): void {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return;\n    // 射程门（:45706 IsInTileInteractionRange Simple 档 + tileBoost）：无底桶/海绵族\n    // tileBoost+=2（Item.cs:28052/:39452/:39648/:42042/:42053/:42534）、5304 +=3（:42062）\n    const tb = bucketVid === 5304 ? 3\n      : (bucketVid === 3031 || bucketVid === 3032 || bucketVid === 4820\n        || bucketVid === 4872 || bucketVid === 5302 || bucketVid === 5303 || bucketVid === 5364) ? 2 : 0;\n    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty, tb)) return;\n    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);\n    // 原版 liquidType()==0 ≡ 水（LiquidID.cs:5）；本仓 +1 编码下 0 视同水\n    const tHere = st.liquidType[i] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER;\n    // 3×3 同类合计（:45723-45731）\n    let sum3x3 = 0;\n    for (let ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {\n      for (let oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(tx + ox, ty + oy)) continue;\n        const j = st.idx(tx + ox, ty + oy);\n        if ((st.liquidType[j] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) === tHere) sum3x3 += st.liquid[j];\n      }\n    }\n    // 倾倒实心门（:45813 nactive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && type!=546 的仓库近似）\n    const td = st.type[i] !== 0 ?            // :45823/:45843/:45863/:45881\n    } else {\n      Game.bucketScoopDrain(st, tx, ty, tHere);             // :45762-45809\n    }\n    this.sfx.play('splash', 1, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8); // SoundID 19（:45760/:45821/:45841/:45861/:45879）\n    if (op.swapTo !== undefined) {\n      // 原版 sItem.stack-- + PutItemInInventoryFromItemUsage：手中原格替换（不跳格）\n      this.player.inv.slots[this.player.inv.selected] = { id: ITEM_BY_KEY[VI(op.swapTo)], stack: 1 };\n      this.cb.onInventoryChanged();\n    }\n    this.player.useTime = op.useTime;                       // ApplyItemTime（:45761/:45830 族）\n  }\n\n  /** 桶/海绵族决策纯函数（Player.cs:45719-45888）——输入目标格状态,输出动作。\n   *  liquidType 用仓库 +1 编码（1 水/2 岩浆/3 蜂蜜/4 微光,0≡水,LiquidID.cs:5-11 +1）。\n   *  kind: none=门拦下 / pour=倾倒 255 / scoop=舀取（含四邻回吸）;useTime=ApplyItemTime 值 */\n  static bucketUse(vid: number, liquid: number, liquidType: number, sum3x3: number,\n    solidBlocked: boolean): { kind: 'none' | 'pour' | 'scoop'; liquidType?: number; swapTo?: number; useTime: number } {\n    const t = liquidType || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER;\n    const isSponge = vid === 3032 || vid === 4872 || vid === 5303 || vid === 5304;\n    // ---- 舀取支入口（:45719）----\n    const scoop = (vid === 205 && t !== LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER)\n      || (vid === 3032 && (t === LIQUID_TYPE.WATER || t === LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER))\n      || (vid === 4872 && t === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA)\n      || (vid === 5303 && t === LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY)\n      || vid === 5304;                                      // 终极海绵任意液体（:45719 末项恒真）\n    if (scoop) {\n      // 门（:45733）：无液体拒绝;3×3 同类合计 ≤100 且非海绵拒绝（海绵可吸浅洼）\n      if (liquid <= 0 || (sum3x3 <= 100 && !isSponge)) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };\n      // 换桶链（:45738-45759）：仅 205——蜜→1128/岩浆→207/水→206（微光已被入口拦）;\n      // 海绵族不消耗（消耗块显式排除 3032/4872/5303/5304）\n      return {\n        kind: 'scoop',\n        swapTo: vid === 205\n          ? (t === LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY ? 1128 : t === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA ? 207 : 206) : undefined,\n        useTime: itemCombat(vid)?.useTime ?? 10,            // ApplyItemTime（:45761）\n      };\n    }\n    // ---- 倾倒支（:45811-45888）----\n    if (liquid >= 200 || solidBlocked) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };  // :45813\n    const pourType: Record<number, number> = {\n      207: LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA, 4820: LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA,       // :45817-45835\n      206: LIQUID_TYPE.WATER, 3031: LIQUID_TYPE.WATER,     // :45837-45855\n      1128: LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY, 5302: LIQUID_TYPE.HONEY,    // :45857-45875\n      5364: LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER,                           // :45877-45888\n    };\n    const lt = pourType[vid];\n    if (lt === undefined) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 }; // 205 在微光上/海绵族不进此支\n    if (liquid > 0 && t !== lt) return { kind: 'none', useTime: 0 };  // 空格或同类才倒\n    // 满桶族回空桶（:45827/:45847/:45867）;无底族 3031/4820/5302/5364 不消耗\n    const bottomless = vid === 3031 || vid === 4820 || vid === 5302 || vid === 5364;\n    return {\n      kind: 'pour',\n      liquidType: lt,\n      swapTo: bottomless ? undefined : 205,\n      useTime: itemCombat(vid)?.useTime ?? 10,              // ApplyItemTime（:45830/:45850/:45870/:45883）\n    };\n  }\n\n  /** 舀取执行（:45762-45809）：清目标格;不足 255 时从 3×3 同类邻居回吸补足,余液\n   *  留在地里。海绵与空桶共用同一舀取体——同样只吸到 255 为止（非清空 3×3）。\n   *  返回舀走总量。 */\n  static bucketScoopDrain(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number, tHere: number): number {\n    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);\n    let got = st.liquid[i];\n    st.setLiquid(tx, ty, 0, 0);\n    if (got >= 255) return got;                             // :45775 目标已满则不回吸\n    for (let ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {\n      for (let oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {\n        if ((ox === 0 && oy === 0) || !st.inBounds(tx + ox, ty + oy)) continue;\n        const j = st.idx(tx + ox, ty + oy);\n        if (st.liquid[j] <= 0 || (st.liquidType[j] || LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) !== tHere) continue;\n        const take = Math.min(st.liquid[j], 255 - got);     // :45785-45788\n        if (take <= 0) continue;                            // 已吸满 255,余格不动（:45786 归零）\n        got += take;\n        st.setLiquid(tx + ox, ty + oy, st.liquid[j] - take, tHere);\n      }\n    }\n    return got;\n  }"
}

273:  constructor(w: number, h: number, seed: number, name = '新世界', skipStore = false) {
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }

Another concern: reach gate. Target at +2 tiles from player — within 5/3 box. Use ty = floor((p.y+p.h)/16)-3 → y offset -3, within 3+boost(2)=5. ✓ For 5304 boost 3 → y range 6. Fine.

1	#!/usr/bin/env node
2	/**
3	 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
4	 *
5	 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
6	 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
7	 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
8	 *
9	 * 用法:
10	 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
11	 *
12	 * 双层架构:
13	 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
14	 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
15	 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
16	 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
17	 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
18	 *
19	 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
20	 */
21	const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
22	const FLUSH_MS = 500;
23	
24	const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
25	
26	// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
27	if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
28	  const target = argv[1];
29	  try {
30	    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
31	    const path = await import('node:path');
32	    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
33	  } catch (e) {
34	    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
35	    process.exit(1);
36	  }
37	  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
38	  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
39	} else {
40	  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
41	  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
42	  const rest = [];
43	  for (const a of argv) {
44	    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
45	      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
46	      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
47	    } else {
48	      rest.push(a);
49	    }
50	  }
51	  if (!rest.length) {
52	    console.error('usage: node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [args...]');
53	    process.exit(64);
54	  }
55	
56	  const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
57	  const path = await import('node:path');
58	  const target = path.resolve(process.cwd(), rest[0]);
59	
60	  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');
61	  const self = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
62	  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [self, '--_child', target, ...rest.slice(1)], {
63	    stdio: 'inherit',
64	  });
65	
66	  let done = false;
67	  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => {
68	    if (done) return;
69	    done = true;
70	    console.error(`[run-diag] 超时 ${timeoutMs}ms,SIGKILL 子进程 (exit 2)`);
71	    child.kill('SIGKILL');
72	    process.exit(2);
73	  }, timeoutMs);
74	
75	  child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
76	    if (done) return;
77	    done = true;
78	    clearTimeout(watchdog);
79	    if (signal) {
80	      console.error(`[run-diag] 子进程被信号 ${signal} 终止 (exit 1)`);
81	      process.exit(1);
82	    }
83	    process.exit(code ?? 1);
84	  });
85	
86	  child.on('error', (e) => {
87	    console.error('[run-diag] 启动失败:', e);
88	    process.exit(1);
89	  });
90	}
91
